BERT-family encoders and zero-shot LLMs proved ineffective on rare classes due to severe class imbalance, leading us to QLoRA fine-tuning of Qwen3-8B. We leverage three key strategies: grouped stratified cross-validation (preventing leakage), minority-class round-robin lexical augmentation, and a post-processing pipeline with logit bias tuning and ensemble blending. Together, these components close much of the validation-to-leaderboard gap and substantially improve minority-class recall, driving the critical "Unclear" class (Level 8) from near-zero performance to an F1 score of 0.797.

</details>


### [110] [FineVerify: Scaling Test-Time Compute with Fine-Grained Self-Verification for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00660)
*James Xu Zhao,Hui Chen,Bryan Hooi,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FineVerify是一个细粒度的自我验证框架，通过将复杂问题分解为可检查的子问题、验证候选答案、并基于聚合得分选择最佳答案，来提升语言模型代理在信息搜索任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于扩大测试时计算的方法在提升语言模型代理的搜索能力时容易失败，因为正确答案通常稀疏且基于评分的筛选依赖于模型的校准能力。

Method: FineVerify将每个问题分解为可检查的子问题，对采样的候选答案针对每个子问题进行验证，然后基于聚合得分选择得分最高的候选答案。这种按检查点的结构将选择转化为更简单的局部判断，并产生基于相同明确标准的分数。

Result: 在四个代理搜索基准测试和两个模型上，FineVerify持续优于标准扩展基线。仅使用四个采样轨迹时，将GPT-5-mini的准确率提升8.2个百分点，Gemini-3-flash平均提升5.6%。使用12个样本时，FineVerify使GPT-5-mini在BrowseComp-Plus上超越前沿的GPT-5。

Conclusion: 除了提升准确率，FineVerify还生成了可解释的验证痕迹，有助于审计基准测试错误，这表明该方法在检查代理搜索系统方面具有更广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Agentic search requires language model agents to explore many sources and answer complex information-seeking questions. Scaling test-time compute is a promising way to improve these agents, but current approaches can fail, because correct answers are often sparse and score-based selection depends on model calibration. We propose FineVerify, a fine-grained self-verification framework that decomposes each question into checkable sub-questions, verifies sampled candidates against each sub-question, and selects the candidate with the highest aggregated score. This per-check structure turns selection into simpler local judgments and produces scores under the same explicit criteria. Across four agentic search benchmarks and two models, FineVerify consistently outperforms standard scaling baselines. With only four sampled trajectories, it improves GPT-5-mini by 8.2 accuracy points and Gemini-3-flash by 5.6% on average. With 12 samples, FineVerify enables GPT-5-mini to surpass frontier GPT-5 on BrowseComp-Plus. Beyond accuracy, FineVerify produces interpretable verification traces that help audit benchmark errors, suggesting broader applications for inspecting agentic search systems. Code and data are available at https://github.com/XuZhao0/fineverify

</details>


### [111] [OCC-RAG: Optimal Cognitive Core for Faithful Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00683)
*Maksim Savkin,Mikhail Goncharov,Alexander Gambashidze,Alla Chepurova,Dmitrii Tarasov,Nikita Andriianov,Daria Pugacheva,Vasily Konovalov,Andrey Galichin,Ivan Oseledets*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OCC-RAG是一个针对基于上下文的忠实问答任务优化的专用小语言模型，通过大规模合成多跳推理数据训练，在多项基准测试中优于大2-6倍的通用途模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型发展过于关注规模扩展和参数知识积累，但许多实际应用更需要稳健的推理能力而非广泛的知识记忆。任务专用的小语言模型为解决这一问题提供了系统化的设计思路。

Method: 提出了最优认知核心（OCC）框架，并专门设计了OCC-RAG变体用于忠实问答。开发了新颖的大规模多上下文、多跳问答数据合成流水线，生成了超过300万个示例的数据集，重点训练多跳推理、严格上下文忠实性和校准性弃权。模型生成带有源引用（基于上下文直接引用）的结构化推理轨迹。

Result: OCC-RAG-0.6B和OCC-RAG-1.7B两个模型在多跳推理（HotpotQA、MuSiQue、TAT-QA）、忠实性（ConFiQA）和弃权（MuSiQue-Un）基准测试中，性能达到或超过比它们大2-6倍的通途语言模型。

Conclusion: 紧凑的任务专用小语言模型在特定推理密集型任务上可以超越更大的通途模型，展示了专业化设计相对于规模扩展的优势，为语言模型开发提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Recent progress in the development of language models has been defined by scale, with each generation absorbing more of the world's knowledge into its weights. However, many practical applications benefit more from robust reasoning than from extensive parametric knowledge. In this setting, task-specialized small language models (SLMs) offer a principled design choice. We introduce Optimal Cognitive Core (OCC), a family of SLMs built around this premise. As a variant of OCC, we present OCC-RAG, optimized for faithful question answering (QA) grounded in the provided context. This task directly aligns with the OCC design approach, requiring multi-hop reasoning over supplied passages while ignoring memorized knowledge. To train OCC-RAG, we implement a novel pipeline for synthesizing multi-context, multi-hop QA data at scale, producing a corpus of over three million examples targeting multi-hop reasoning, strict context faithfulness, and calibrated abstention. We release OCC-RAG-0.6B and OCC-RAG-1.7B, both mid-trained on this corpus. The models produce structured reasoning traces with source citations grounded in literal quotes from the context. Through OCC-RAG, we demonstrate that compact, task-specialized SLMs can match or exceed general-purpose models 2 -- 6x their size across multi-hop reasoning (HotpotQA, MuSiQue, TAT-QA), faithfulness (ConFiQA), and refusal (MuSiQue-Un) benchmarks.

</details>


### [112] [EPIC: Efficient and Parallel Inference under CFG Constraints for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00722)
*Hyundong Jin,Yo-Sub Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EPIC：一种高效的上下文无关文法约束的扩散语言模型解码框架，通过词法记忆化、Earley式解析和宽松兼容子集选择等技术，将推理时间减少高达67.5%，额外开销降低高达90.5%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的上下文无关文法（CFG）约束扩散语言模型解码方法存在显著效率问题：速度比无约束解码慢4倍，且削弱了扩散模型相对于自回归模型的关键优势——并行解码能力。这是由于并行生成过程中顺序有效性检查引入了大量开销。

Method: 提出EPIC框架，结合三种关键技术：1）词法记忆化减少重复词法分析；2）使用Earley式解析代替确定性自动机进行验证；3）宽松兼容子集选择，允许多个兼容标记在并行提交中共同提交。

Result: 在三个基准测试和四个模型上的实验表明：相比现有CFG约束解码方法，EPIC将推理时间减少高达67.5%，额外开销降低高达90.5%，显著提升了效率。

Conclusion: EPIC框架有效解决了CFG约束扩散语言模型解码的效率瓶颈问题，通过创新性的词法记忆化、高效解析和并行提交策略，在保持约束满足的同时大幅提升了解码速度，恢复了扩散语言模型的并行解码优势。

Abstract: Controlling language model outputs is essential for ensuring structural validity, reliability, and downstream usability, and diffusion language models are no exception. Recent advances in diffusion language model decoding have extended output control beyond regular constraints to context-free grammar (CFG) constraints. Existing methods, however, can be up to four times slower than unconstrained decoding. More importantly, they substantially diminish one of the key advantages of diffusion language models over autoregressive models, namely parallel decoding. This slowdown arises because sequential validity checking introduces significant overhead during parallel generation. We propose an efficient CFG-constrained decoding framework, EPIC, that addresses this limitation. Our method improves decoding efficiency by combining lexing memoization, validation using Earley-style parsing instead of deterministic automata, and relaxed compatible subset selection for parallel commit. It reduces repeated lexing and validation overhead while allowing multiple compatible tokens to be committed together. Experiments on three benchmarks using four models show that our method reduces inference time by up to 67.5% and decreases the additional overhead by up to 90.5% compared with existing CFG-constrained decoding methods. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/hyundong98/EPIC-Decoding.git .

</details>


### [113] [WaveFilter: Enhancing the Long-Context Capability of Diffusion LLMs via Wavelet-Guided KV Cache Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00724)
*Jinnan Yang,Yan Wang,Zhen Bi,Kehao Wu,Xiaojie Li,Jungang Lou,Zechao Li,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WaveFilter是一个无需训练的通用KV缓存框架，利用小波变换分解长序列来精确识别关键token，通过构建稀疏KV Cache来提升扩散语言模型在长上下文任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型(DLMs)在长上下文任务中面临计算开销和推理延迟的瓶颈。现有的KV缓存机制在处理长序列时常面临生成质量急剧下降的困境，核心挑战在于精确高效地过滤超长上下文中的关键token。

Method: 受人类阅读过程启发，提出WaveFilter框架，创新性地引入小波变换对长序列进行分解，实现关键token的精确识别，并基于此构建稀疏KV Cache来计算最终的上下文表示。

Result: 实验结果表明，WaveFilter作为一个即插即用的通用框架，在复杂的长上下文任务中显著提升了现有主流KV缓存方法的性能。

Conclusion: WaveFilter通过小波变换实现精确的关键token识别，为解决扩散语言模型在长上下文任务中的计算瓶颈提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (DLMs) have demonstrated significant advantages across various tasks. However, constrained by their multi-step iterative inference mechanism, their computational overhead and inference latency in long-context tasks have become core bottlenecks restricting their large-scale deployment. When processing long sequences, existing Key-Value (KV) caching mechanisms often face a dilemma where generation quality degrades drastically, where the core challenge lies in precisely and efficiently filtering critical tokens within ultra-long contexts. Inspired by the human reading process, we propose \textbf{WaveFilter}, a universal and training-free caching framework. This framework innovatively introduces the wavelet transform for decomposition of long sequences to achieve precise identification of key tokens, based on which a sparse KV Cache is constructed to compute the final contextual representation. Experimental results demonstrate that WaveFilter, as a plug-and-play generic framework, significantly enhances the performance of existing mainstream KV Cache methods in complex long-context tasks.

</details>


### [114] [From Empathy to Personalized Empathy: Adapting Empathetic Strategies to Individual Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00728)
*Wuqiang Zheng,Chengbing Wang,Yilin Yang,Junyi Cheng,Jianfei Xiao,Hu Sun,Yi Xie,Yangyang Li,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出个性化同理心任务，通过PersonaEmp数据集和PereGRM奖励建模框架，根据用户历史个性化特征调整同理心策略，显著提升LLMs的个性化同理心能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型在长期用户交互中的部署增多，同理心能力变得日益重要。现有研究忽视了长期交互中用户个性特征对同理心策略的影响，因此需要研究个性化同理心任务。

Method: 1) 构建PersonaEmp数据集：基于长期用户-AI交互，包含丰富用户历史、角色信息和同理心寻求查询；2) 提出PereGRM奖励建模框架：结合同理心评估结构和动态评估标准生成，进行细粒度奖励建模。

Result: 在不同设置和多个评判模型下的实验结果显示，PereGRM持续实现最强的性能提升，表明其在增强个性化同理心能力方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 个性化同理心是一个重要研究任务，通过PersonaEmp数据集和PereGRM框架可以显著提升LLMs在长期交互中根据用户个性特征调整同理心策略的能力，为未来的个性化AI交互研究提供了基础。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in long-term interactions with users, empathy has become an increasingly important capability. However, existing research overlooks the influence of users' personality traits on empathetic strategies during long-term interactions. To address this gap, we introduce the task of personalized empathy, which focuses on adapting empathetic strategies according to users' personalized characteristics derived from history. To study and enhance this capability, we construct PersonaEmp, a personalized empathy dataset built from long-term user-AI interactions, featuring rich user histories, persona information, and empathy-seeking queries. We further propose PereGRM, a reward modeling framework that combines the empathy evaluation structure with dynamic evaluation criteria generation for fine-grained reward modeling. Experimental results across different settings and multiple judge models show that PereGRM consistently achieves the strongest performance improvements, indicating its effectiveness for enhancing personalized empathetic capabilities.

</details>


### [115] [I-WebGenBench : Evaluating Interactivity in LLM-Generated Scientific Web Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00750)
*Dasen Dai,Biao Wu,Meng Fang,Shuoqi Li,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PaperVoyager：一种将研究论文自动转换为可执行交互式网页系统的AI代理，通过端到端处理实现动态机制的可视化与交互


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档代理主要将论文转换为摘要、网页或幻灯片等静态产物，不足以处理涉及动态机制和状态转换的技术论文。需要一种能够将论文转换为交互式可操作系统的方法，让用户可以操纵输入并观察动态行为。

Method: 提出了PaperVoyager框架，这是一种结构化生成框架，在合成过程中显式建模机制和交互逻辑。给定PDF论文，代理执行端到端处理，包括论文理解、系统建模和交互式网页合成，无需人工干预。

Result: 为评估该任务，引入了包含19篇研究论文与专家构建交互式系统作为真实标注的基准。实验表明，PaperVoyager显著提高了生成交互式系统的质量，为交互式科学论文理解提供了新范式。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种将研究论文转换为可执行交互式Web系统的AI代理，通过显式建模动态机制和交互逻辑，为科学论文理解提供了新的交互范式，实现了从静态文档到动态可操作系统的转变。

Abstract: Recent advances in visual language models have enabled autonomous agents for complex reasoning, tool use, and document understanding. However, existing document agents mainly transform papers into static artifacts such as summaries, webpages, or slides, which are insufficient for technical papers involving dynamic mechanisms and state transitions. In this work, we propose a Paper-to-Interactive-System Agent that converts research papers into executable interactive web systems. Given a PDF paper, the agent performs end-to-end processing without human intervention, including paper understanding, system modeling, and interactive webpage synthesis, enabling users to manipulate inputs and observe dynamic behaviors. To evaluate this task, we introduce a benchmark of 19 research papers paired with expert-built interactive systems as ground truth. We further propose PaperVoyager, a structured generation framework that explicitly models mechanisms and interaction logic during synthesis. Experiments show that PaperVoyager significantly improves the quality of generated interactive systems, offering a new paradigm for interactive scientific paper understanding.

</details>


### [116] [Internalize the Temperature: On-Policy Self-Distillation as Policy Reheater for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00755)
*Xuewei Yang,Jiachen Yu,Jie Wu,Shaoning Sun,Junjie Wang,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TS-OPSD是一种轻量级策略再加热方法，通过在模型参数内部化温度探索效应，解决强化学习中熵崩溃问题，无需外部教师或额外推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习虽然能提升大语言模型的推理能力，但常遭受熵崩溃问题，即策略越来越集中导致rollout多样性减少和学习信号减弱。现有方法要么约束RL目标（如熵正则化），要么在rollout收集期间调整采样温度，但这些干预都停留在模型参数之外。

Method: 提出温度缩放的策略内自蒸馏（TS-OPSD），从熵崩溃的RL检查点开始，通过将高温度缩放应用于模型自身的logits构建自教师，然后将得到的更平滑分布蒸馏回学生模型。这种方法无需外部教师、特权数据或额外推理成本。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Base和Qwen3-8B-Base上的实验表明，策略再加热作为继续RL的初始化比标准继续RL和rollout级温度再加热都更强。分析显示TS-OPSD主要减少输出锐度，同时保留中间表示、候选集和推理能力。

Conclusion: 熵恢复可作为简单的后崩溃干预方法，用于扩展面向推理的强化学习。TS-OPSD通过内部化温度探索效应，有效缓解熵崩溃问题，为RL训练提供了更好的初始化策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards improves the reasoning ability of large language models, but often suffers from entropy collapse, in which increasingly concentrated policies reduce rollout diversity and useful learning signals. Existing remedies either constrain the RL objective (e.g., entropy regularization) or adjust sampling temperature during rollout collection, but these interventions remain external to the model parameters. We propose Temperature-Scaled On-Policy Self-Distillation (TS-OPSD), a lightweight policy reheating method that internalizes the exploratory effect of temperature into model parameters. Starting from an entropy-collapsed RL checkpoint, TS-OPSD constructs a self-teacher by applying high-temperature scaling to the model's own logits, then distills the resulting smoother distribution back into the student. This policy reheating requires no external teacher, privileged data, or additional inference cost. Experiments on Qwen3-4B-Base and Qwen3-8B-Base show that policy reheating yields a stronger initialization for continued RL than both standard continued RL and rollout-level temperature reheating. Further analyses show that TS-OPSD mainly reduces output sharpness while preserving intermediate representations, top candidate sets, and reasoning capability. These results suggest that entropy restoration can serve as a simple post-collapse intervention for extending reasoning-oriented RL.

</details>


### [117] [Not All Flips Are Conformity: Decomposing Stance Convergence in Multi-Agent LLM Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00820)
*Xiqi Hao,Zengqing Wu,Yu-Xuan Qiu,Chuan Xiao,Ruiqi Xu,Shuyuan Zheng,Jianbin Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个三源分解框架来区分多智能体辩论中的三种机制：自发不稳定性、立场诱导的从众性和推理诱导的说服性，揭示了传统答案翻转率指标的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体辩论是改进大语言模型推理能力的有前景策略，但智能体在达成共识时难以区分是真正的深思熟虑还是社会从众。传统答案翻转率指标混淆了三种不同机制，需要更精细的分析框架。

Method: 提出了三源分解框架，通过控制反事实条件来隔离每种机制：1) 自发不稳定性（仅自我反思），2) 立场诱导的从众性，3) 推理诱导的说服性。采用受控信息梯度实验，并在MMLU-Pro、GPQA-Diamond等数据集上进行鲁棒性测试。

Result: 在主要MMLU-Pro设置中，37%的智能体-问题观察仅因自我反思而改变；严格从众性占29%，且主要是有害的（57-77%从正确变为错误）；即使空洞推理也会导致20-39%的错误采纳率。有害从众性可从第0轮特征预测（AUC=0.79），风险目标干预可减少13.6个百分点（p<0.001）。但若无正确性标签或自我反思控制，减少同伴采纳不会提高准确性。

Conclusion: 多智能体辩论中的共识可能更多反映社会从众而非真正推理。需要区分辩论中的不同机制，单纯减少同伴采纳而不考虑正确性方向会适得其反。研究为理解和改进多智能体辩论系统提供了实证基础和理论框架。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate (MAD) is a promising strategy for improving LLM reasoning, but when agents converge on a shared answer, it is unclear whether that convergence reflects genuine deliberation or social compliance. We show that the conventional answer flip rate conflates three distinct mechanisms: spontaneous instability, stance-induced conformity, and reasoning-induced persuasion. Our three-source decomposition framework isolates each through controlled counterfactual conditions. In the primary MMLU-Pro setting, 37% of agent-question observations change under self-reflection alone, while robustness tests show substantial model-dependent instability across GPQA-Diamond and three model families; strict conformity is 29% in the primary setting and remains predominantly harmful across model replications (57-77% correct-to-wrong). A controlled information-gradient experiment reveals that even vacuous reasoning is associated with 20-39% error adoption among resistant agents, with reasoning-like presentation carrying substantial persuasive weight. Harmful conformity can be predicted from Round 0 features (AUC = 0.79), and risk-targeted intervention reduces it by 13.6 percentage points (p < 0.001). However, without correctness labels or self-reflection controls, reducing peer adoption does not improve accuracy, because harmful and beneficial influence cannot be distinguished.

</details>


### [118] [Momento: Evaluating Persistent Memory and Reasoning with Multi-Session Agentic Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00832)
*Adril Putra Merin,David Anugraha,Ayu Purwarianti,Genta Indra Winata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Momento是一个面向多会话服务环境的持久智能体任务完成基准测试，要求智能体处理跨会话的时序依赖和用户目标演化，当前智能体主要因用户状态误估计而失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试仅评估单会话内的性能，忽略了智能体需要整合历史动作、用户偏好和先前决策来满足个性化用户目标这一现实需求。

Method: 引入Momento基准测试，该基准模拟多会话服务环境，要求智能体在跨会话中执行具有后果的工具中介动作，同时处理时序依赖和用户目标的演化。

Result: 实验结果显示，当前智能体主要通过误估计用户状态而失败，它们倾向于将先前的会话历史视为可靠代理而非需要重新验证的过时信息。

Conclusion: 当前智能体能力与现实的长周期人-智能体交互之间存在显著差距，现有智能体无法有效处理跨会话的持久性任务需求。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic AI have enabled agents to complete complex tasks through tool use, reasoning, and multi-step planning. Yet existing benchmarks evaluate agents within a single session, ignoring past actions, stated preferences, and prior decisions that agents must integrate to fulfill personalized user goals. We introduce Momento, a benchmark for persistent agentic task completion in multi-session service environments, requiring agents to take consequential, tool-mediated actions while resolving temporal dependencies and evolving user goals across sessions. Experimental results reveal that current agents fail primarily through misestimation of user state, treating prior session history as a reliable proxy for current context rather than stale information requiring re-validation, highlighting a substantial gap between current agent capabilities and realistic long-horizon human-agent interaction.

</details>


### [119] [IDEAFix: Evaluation Framework for Creative Defixation Prompting in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00875)
*F. Carichon,S. Sharma,M. Girard,R. Rampa,G. Farnadi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IDEAFix评估框架系统分析大语言模型在开放式创意生成任务中的发散思维，通过控制变量（场景、任务属性、去固定化策略）揭示结构化提示对创意输出的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM创意能力评估存在不足：要么使用脱离情境的狭隘任务，要么在更广泛的设置中混淆创意过程的多个方面，难以隔离任务表述、提示和评估设计的影响。结构化提示策略在创意生成中的作用尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 引入IDEAFix评估框架，通过让模型为受控变体的短期设计场景生成多个原创解决方案，系统分析任务表述、属性选择和去固定化提示策略如何影响LLM的创意生成。

Result: 任务表述和属性选择显著影响模型性能，简单提示策略能提升解决方案的原创性；但也观察到模型间持续的输出同质化现象，证实其在生成多样化解决方案方面存在固有局限。

Conclusion: IDEAFix提供了一个受控、可扩展的框架来研究LLM创意机制的基础原理，揭示了结构化指导对创意生成的重要性，同时确认了当前LLM在创意多样性方面的内在限制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for tasks involving creative problem solving and idea generation. However, there is a lack of consensus concerning their creative capabilities: some studies report superior performances compared to humans, while others highlight structural limitations such as fixation and the homogenization of outputs. Existing evaluation approaches either rely on narrow, decontextualized tasks that do not capture goal-oriented generation or on broader settings that confound multiple aspects of the creative process, making it difficult to isolate the effects of task formulation, prompting, and evaluation design. Significantly, the role of structured prompting strategies in shaping idea generation remains underexplored. Therefore, we introduce IDEAFix, an evaluation framework for analyzing divergent thinking in open-ended idea generation tasks. We prompt models to generate multiple original solutions to controlled variations of short design scenarios, task attributes, and defixation prompting strategies. This design enables systematic analysis of how structured guidance influences LLMs' idea generation. Our results show that both task formulation and attribute selection significantly affect models' performance, and that simple prompting strategies can boost the originality of solutions. However, we also observe persistent output homogenization across models, confirming inherent limits in their ability to generate diverse solutions. Overall, IDEAFix provides a controlled, extensible framework for studying the mechanisms underlying LLMs' creativity.

</details>


### [120] [Chunking Methods on Retrieval-Augmented Generation - Effectiveness Evaluation Against Computational Cost and Limitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00881)
*Mateusz Śmigielski,Michał Rajkowski,Mateusz Zbrocki,Michał Bernacki-Janson,Karol Kunicki,Julianna Godziszewska,Maciej Piasecki,Konrad Wojtasik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次对RAG系统中多种文本切分方法进行了系统评估，揭示了传统固定尺寸和语义切分的局限性，并强调切分策略常被低估的实际挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统性能提升的关键在于文本切分策略。虽然近年来涌现了许多新的切分方法，但这些方法多为特定用例设计，缺乏跨场景有效性证据，难以直接比较不同技术的优劣。

Method: 首次系统性地评估了广泛的文本切分方法，深入分析了RAG系统中切分策略所面临的潜在挑战。将传统上被视为简单预处理步骤的切分过程重新定义为需要仔细考虑的关键环节。

Result: 研究发现文本切分引入了一系列重要但常被忽视的问题。对比分析了固定尺寸切分、语义切分等传统方法与新兴方法的相对优势和局限性。

Conclusion: 文本切分不仅仅是预处理步骤，而是影响RAG系统性能的关键因素。需要更系统的评估框架来比较不同切分方法，推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has demonstrated significant capabilities in enhancing the performance of Large Language Models (LLMs). One of the key tasks in RAG systems is the chunking process. Traditionally, fixed-size chunking and semantic chunking have been the standard approaches. However, interest in chunking strategies has been increasing, leading to a growing number of proposed methods that often claim improved performance over these conventional techniques. Many of these approaches are tailored to specific use cases and data types, with limited evidence of their effectiveness across diverse scenarios. As a result, it remains challenging to directly compare different techniques and assess their relative strengths. To the best of our knowledge, this study is the first to systematically evaluate the effectiveness of a wide range of chunking methods and emphasize the underlying challenges of chunking strategies in RAG systems. While chunking is commonly treated as a simple preprocessing step, we show that it introduces a range of impactful and often overlooked issues.

</details>


### [121] [Citation Grounding: Detecting and Reducing LLM Citation Hallucinations via Legal Citation Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00898)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了引用接地(CG)指标来测量和减少LLM在生成法律引用时的幻觉问题，并引入CG-DPO方法通过算法构建偏好对来微调模型，在乌克兰法律案例上验证了效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在法律引用方面存在系统性幻觉问题（编造法规引用、引用已废止条款、混淆管辖权等），但现有方法缺乏在大规模上自动测量和减少这种行为的有效手段

Method: 1. 提出引用接地(CG)指标，基于从1.008亿份乌克兰法院判决中提取的真实引用图（5.02亿条边，21,736个独特法规节点）验证LLM生成的法律引用。2. CG分解为三个组件：引用精度（引用条款是否存在？）、引用相关性（是否上下文合适？）和引用时间性（在相关日期是否有效？）。3. 引入引用接地DPO(CG-DPO)方法，通过四种针对性策略算法性地构建偏好对，使用来自真实法院判决的已验证引用来训练模型

Result: 1. 在100个乌克兰法律查询上对五个系统（四个商业LLM和一个RAG增强生产系统）的评估显示CG值在0.791到0.873之间，13-21%的引用存在幻觉。2. 在2,244个法院判决数据集上，使用LoRA微调的Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型在区分正确与受损引用方面达到98.5%的平均验证准确率（奖励边际+14.9，3个种子下标准差<0.3pp）

Conclusion: 提出的CG指标和CG-DPO方法能有效测量和减少LLM在法律引用中的幻觉问题，为自动化法律引用验证提供了可行方案，相关数据集和框架已作为开放资源发布

Abstract: Large language models systematically hallucinate legal citations -- fabricating statute references, citing repealed provisions, and confusing jurisdictions -- yet no automated method exists to measure or reduce this behavior at scale. We propose citation grounding (CG), a metric that verifies LLM-generated legal citations against a ground-truth citation graph extracted from 100.8 million Ukrainian court decisions (502 million edges, 21,736 unique statute nodes). CG decomposes into three components -- citation precision (does the cited provision exist?), citation relevance (is it contextually appropriate?), and citation temporality (was it valid at the relevant date?) -- enabling differential diagnosis of hallucination types. Empirical evaluation on 100 Ukrainian legal queries across five systems -- four commercial LLMs via AWS Bedrock (Claude Haiku 4.5, Mistral Pixtral Large, Amazon Nova Pro/Lite) and one RAG-augmented production system -- reveals CG ranging from 0.791 to 0.873, with 13-21% of citations hallucinated. To reduce hallucinations without human annotation, we introduce Citation Grounding DPO (CG-DPO): a method that constructs preference pairs algorithmically by corrupting verified citations from real court decisions via four targeted strategies. On a dataset of 2,244 court decisions, a Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct model fine-tuned with LoRA achieves 98.5% mean validation accuracy in distinguishing correct from corrupted citations (rewards margin +14.9, std < 0.3 pp across 3 seeds). The citation graph, evaluation framework, and CG-DPO dataset are released as open resources.

</details>


### [122] [MLLM-Microscope: Unlocking Hidden Structure Within Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00909)
*Ravil Mussabayev,Rustam Mussabayev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MLLM-Microscope系统分析多模态大语言模型的隐藏表示特性，发现不同模型在token线性度、内在维度和各向异性方面存在显著差异，这些差异与模态融合方式密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了深入理解多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的内部工作机制，特别是不同模态融合方式对模型表示特性的影响，需要系统分析其隐藏表示的特性。

Method: 开发MLLM-Microscope系统，在ScienceQA数据集上评估LLaVA-NeXT和OmniFusion两种先进MLLM，分析多模态token嵌入在transformer层中的线性度、内在维度和各向异性。

Result: 1) 两种模态的主流和残差流token在所有层都表现出高度线性行为；2) LLaVA-NeXT的图像token线性度略有下降，而OmniFusion保持稳定；3) OmniFusion的图像token维度在各层均高于LLaVA-NeXT；4) OmniFusion的各向异性在各层保持较低水平。

Conclusion: MLLM的内部工作机制高度依赖于在将token序列输入LLM之前执行的模态融合性质，这些发现有助于增强对MLLM内部工作机制的理解，指导未来模型设计和优化。

Abstract: This work presents MLLM-Microscope, a novel system designed for analyzing the hidden representations within Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Our system evaluates the linearity, intrinsic dimension, and anisotropy of multimodal token embeddings across transformer layers. Utilizing the ScienceQA dataset, we evaluate two state-of-the-art MLLMs, LLaVA-NeXT and OmniFusion. We find that both the main and residual streams for tokens of both modalities exhibit highly linear behaviors across transformer layers. However, LLaVA-NeXT's image tokens reveal a slight decline in linearity, whereas OmniFusion's remain consistent. Image token dimensions in OmniFusion remain consistently higher across layers compared to LLaVA-NeXT. Also, the OmniFusion's anisotropy is observed to stay consistently low throughout the layers. These findings suggest that the inner workings of MLLMs highly depend on the nature of modality fusion performed before passing the token sequence into LLM. This and other new potential insights obtainable from our system are surely capable of enhancing our understanding of the inner workings of MLLMs, informing future model design and optimization.

</details>


### [123] [Towards Lightweight Reliability: Using Soft Prompts for Hallucination Mitigation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00919)
*S M Tahmid Siddiqui,Akib Jawad Ononto,Anoop Singhal,Latifur Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种参数高效的软提示方法RCSP，通过对比损失、课程学习和KL正则化来平衡抑制幻觉、鼓励不确定时弃权、保持事实召回三个目标，在多个生成式QA数据集上取得优于基线方法的F分数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在广泛应用中面临幻觉问题，即生成听起来合理但事实错误的内容，这在高风险领域会降低信任并引入现实风险，需要解决这一问题以提升模型可靠性

Method: 提出Responsible Contrastive Soft Prompting (RCSP)方法，使用复合损失训练软提示，结合对比损失、课程学习和KL正则化来抑制幻觉内容、鼓励不确定时弃权、同时保持或改善事实召回能力

Result: 在Gemma 3(12B)和Llama 3.1(8B)模型上评估五个生成式QA数据集，使用LLM-as-a-Judge框架，RCSP在平衡事实召回与幻觉抑制和弃权方面表现优于标准推理和基于指令的提示基线，且仅训练少量参数

Conclusion: 软提示提供了一种模块化且计算高效的路径来改善LLM可靠性，RCSP方法通过参数高效的方式有效缓解了幻觉问题并促进了负责任弃权

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have seen widespread adoption across various domains, yet their reliability is frequently undermined by hallucinations - responses that are plausible-sounding but factually incorrect. In high-stakes domains, these errors can reduce trust and introduce real-world risk. To address this challenge, we present a parameter-efficient approach that uses soft prompts to mitigate hallucinated content and promote responsible abstention in generative question-answering (QA) tasks.
  Our method, called Responsible Contrastive Soft Prompting (RCSP), uses a composite loss to train soft prompts that balance three goals: suppressing hallucinatory content, encouraging abstention under uncertainty, and preserving or improving factual recall. To achieve these goals, we incorporate contrastive loss, curriculum learning, and KL regularization into our training mechanism. We evaluate our approach on five diverse generative QA datasets using an LLM-as-a-Judge framework. Experimental results on the Gemma 3 (12B) and Llama 3.1 (8B) backbones demonstrate that RCSP effectively balances factual recall with hallucination suppression and abstention, yielding a generally superior F-score over standard reasoning and instruction-based prompting baselines. Notably, these improvements are achieved by training only a fraction of the parameters required by other tuning techniques. Our results demonstrate that soft prompts provide a modular and computationally efficient path toward improving LLM reliability.

</details>


### [124] [Detection vs. Execution: Single-Bucket Probes Miss Half the Mamba-2 State Sink](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00930)
*Yuhang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现Mamba-2中探测识别出的表征签名不一定对应执行电路，存在执行层与检测层的功能分离。通过BOS专用头的消融实验证明，表征相似性不等于功能等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机制可解释性通常假设探测识别出的表征签名能够识别执行相应计算的电路。本文质疑这一假设在Mamba-2模型中可能系统性失效，旨在揭示表征相似性与功能等价性之间的差异。

Method: 研究Mamba-2中的状态下沉现象，使用单桶探测和多类聚合探测识别不同功能头组；通过类条件消融实验（而非类条件余弦相似性）区分执行电路与检测电路；设计随机通道分组对照实验。

Result: 1. 状态下沉可分解为两个功能头组：BOS专用头（约5%的头）对BOS上下文和换行目标预测具有因果支持作用；双头（27-35%的头）显示更强的表征相似性但消融效果较弱。2. 消融BOS专用头导致RULER NIAH检索准确率从1.00降至0.00，而大小匹配的对照组保持基准性能。3. 随机通道分组对照排除了底物粒度的影响，暗示Mamba-2头共享Delta投影的作用。

Conclusion: 表征相似性不代表功能等价性；探测可以识别执行电路，但在粗粒度下同一探测也会恢复检测电路；分离执行与检测电路需要类条件消融而非类条件余弦相似性分析。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability often assumes that probes identifying a representational signature also identify the circuit executing the corresponding computation. We show that this assumption can fail systematically in Mamba-2. Studying the state sink (disproportionate Delta-gate activation on boundary tokens, analogous to the attention sink), we find that single-bucket probes recover only a small execution layer while missing a much larger detection layer with the same representational signature.
  In Mamba-2, the state sink decomposes into two functional head sets. Single-bucket BOS-specialist heads (about 5% of heads at 2.7B) causally support both BOS-context and newline-target predictions across model scales and corpora. Dual heads (27-35% of heads, recovered by multi-class aggregation of the same probe) show stronger BOS-newline representational similarity but substantially weaker causal effects under ablation. Representational similarity does not imply functional equivalence.
  This distinction matters for downstream behaviour: ablating BOS-specialist heads collapses RULER NIAH retrieval accuracy from 1.00 to 0.00 at 1024 context length in both Mamba-1 2.8B and Mamba-2 2.7B, while size-matched complements preserve baseline performance. A random channel-bucketing control rules out substrate granularity alone, implicating Mamba-2's head-shared Delta projection. Probe-derived specialty can identify execution circuits; at coarse granularity the same probe also recovers detection circuits, and separating them requires class-conditional ablation rather than class-conditional cosine.

</details>


### [125] [HypothesisMed: Inference-Time Answer Fusion and Structured Hypothesis-Space Reporting for Biomedical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00971)
*Md Motaleb Hossen Manik,Ge Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HypothesisMed：一个用于生物医学多项选择题回答的推理时可靠性管道，通过结合多种提示方法和答案融合来改善模型输出质量，同时提供结构化可靠性评估（SPACE标签）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生物医学问答中，仅使用答案准确性评估大语言模型存在不足，因为它不能反映模型是否能够：1）生成可解析的输出；2）遵循结构化可靠性指令；3）识别弱答案空间；4）避免自信的错误承诺。

Method: 提出了HypothesisMed推理时可靠性管道，结合直接提示、思维链、HypothesisMed-v3提示和答案融合。HypothesisMed-v3提供SPACE标签（VALID、INCOMPLETE、CONTRADICTED）和置信度信息，最终答案通过融合选择。

Result: 在MedQA、MedMCQA和PubMedQA数据集上评估Qwen2.5-7B、Phi-4-mini、DeepSeek-R1-32B和BioMistral-7B（每个数据集1000例）。管道提高了加权准确性，增加了解析和SPACE覆盖。扩大评估规模显示：Phi-4-mini准确性从0.4296提升至0.5192；Qwen2.5-7B融合实现了完整的解析和SPACE覆盖，且错误承诺率更低。SPACE压力测试显示SPACE准确性仍较低（Qwen2.5-7B：0.3074，Phi-4-mini：0.4168）。

Conclusion: 研究表明答案准确性、可解析性、结构化可靠性报告、校准行为和错误承诺行为是可分离的能力。主要贡献不是提出一个通用的最优方法，而是提供一个可复现的推理时框架，用于在结构化可靠性约束下评估生物医学问答模型作为可审计的工作流组件。

Abstract: Biomedical question answering with large language models is commonly evaluated using answer accuracy, but answer accuracy alone does not indicate whether a model can produce parseable outputs, follow structured reliability instructions, recognize weak answer spaces, or avoid confident incorrect commitments. This paper presents HypothesisMed, an inference-time reliability pipeline for biomedical multiple-choice question answering. It combines direct, chain-of-thought, HypothesisMed-v3 prompting, and answer fusion. The final answer is selected by fusion, while HypothesisMed-v3 supplies SPACE labels and confidence information. SPACE labels mark the answer space as VALID, INCOMPLETE, or CONTRADICTED. We evaluate Qwen2.5-7B, Phi-4-mini, DeepSeek-R1-32B, and BioMistral-7B on MedQA, MedMCQA, and PubMedQA using 1,000 examples per dataset. The pipeline improves weighted accuracy over each model's best direct or chain-of-thought baseline while increasing parse and SPACE coverage. We also scale evaluation to Qwen2.5-7B and Phi-4-mini using 10,183 examples per model. Fusion improves Phi-4-mini accuracy from 0.4296 to 0.5192, while Qwen2.5-7B chain-of-thought remains slightly higher in answer accuracy. However, Qwen2.5-7B fusion achieves complete parse and SPACE coverage with much lower false commitment. A 12,000-example SPACE stress test shows answer-space diagnosis remains difficult, with SPACE accuracy of 0.3074 for Qwen2.5-7B and 0.4168 for Phi-4-mini. These results show that answer accuracy, parseability, structured reliability reporting, calibration behavior, and false-commitment behavior are separable capabilities. The main contribution is not a universal state-of-the-art claim, but a reproducible inference-time framework for evaluating biomedical question answering models as auditable workflow components under structured reliability constraints.

</details>


### [126] [Lost in Delusion: Examining LLM Safety Under User Delusions and Distress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00975)
*Andrew Aquilina,Chetna Nihalani,Vasudha Varadarajan,Nathan S. Fishbein,Yu-Ru Lin,Maarten Sap*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM聊天机器人在面对与妄想信念交织的心理困扰时存在识别-干预差距：虽然能识别困扰，但一旦困扰被嵌入妄想框架，安全干预措施会大幅减少


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM聊天机器人越来越多地作为心理困扰人群的首选支持来源，但先前研究主要评估一般治疗效果或单轮危机检测，不清楚模型在长期对话中面对与妄想交织的困扰时的表现

Method: 采用匹配的多轮模拟方法，基于临床基础设计人格角色，在六个LLM上运行，每个妄想对话都配对一个仅困扰的对照对话，以隔离妄想框架的影响

Result: 发现识别-干预差距：模型在不同框架下识别困扰的比率相当，但一旦困扰被嵌入妄想框架，安全干预措施被抑制多达4.5倍；失败源于对用户前提的累积接受而非情绪验证；直觉的提示调整策略在妄想框架下适得其反

Conclusion: 安全部署需要将妄想框架视为一个独特风险信号，优先于对话迁就；只有具有明确响应指导的妄想意识提示才能弥补差距，而这依赖于妄想分类器本身在最脆弱模型上的不可靠性

Abstract: LLM chatbots increasingly serve as a first source of support for people in psychological distress, including those whose distress is entangled with delusional beliefs. Prior work on LLM mental-health safety largely evaluates general therapeutic quality or single-turn crisis detection, leaving unclear how models behave when distress is intertwined with delusion over sustained conversations. We address this gap with matched multi-turn simulations, across clinically grounded personas and six LLMs, that pair each delusional conversation with a distress-only control to isolate the effect of delusional framing. This reveals a recognition-intervention gap: models detect distress at comparable rates regardless of framing, yet sharply fail to act on it once distress is embedded in delusion, with safety interventions suppressed by up to 4.5x. The failure tracks accumulated acceptance of the user's premises rather than emotional validation. Worse, the intuitive fix of prompting models to assess user distress backfires under delusional framing; only delusion-aware prompting with explicit response guidance closes the gap, and even this depends on a delusion classifier that is itself unreliable on the most vulnerable models. Safe deployment therefore requires treating delusional framing as a distinct risk signal that overrides conversational accommodation.

</details>


### [127] [Robust Asynchronous Planning via Auto-Formalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00981)
*Jiayi Zhang,Jianing Yin,Ben Zhou,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs在异步规划任务中，形式化表示选择是关键：CP-SAT形式化方法在依赖图从5到100动作的扩展中保持94%准确率，远优于直接规划（从96%降至5%）和PDDL2.1形式化（从13%降至0%）。执行时间约束更新进一步降低性能，但状态感知修复策略可将CP-SAT恢复至84.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数真实世界规划任务都具有异步性、非均匀持续时间、并发性和执行时间约束等特性，但现有基准测试很少覆盖这些复杂场景。需要统一这些异步规划挑战并建立相应的评估基准。

Method: 1) 提出统一框架将异步规划挑战形式化；2) 引入首个三个大规模基准测试分别针对不同异步规划挑战；3) 比较LLMs作为直接规划器(Planner)和形式化器(Formalizer)两种方法的性能；4) 评估不同形式化表示(PDDL2.1与CP-SAT)的效果；5) 提出状态感知修复策略处理执行时间约束更新。

Result: 实验显示：1) 随着依赖图从5到100动作扩展，直接规划器准确率从96%降至5%；2) PDDL2.1形式化从13%降至0%；3) CP-SAT形式化平均保持94%，在100动作时仍有83%；4) 执行时间约束更新使性能进一步下降（直接规划23.9%，PDDL2.1 0.7%，CP-SAT 46.1%）；5) 状态感知修复策略可将CP-SAT恢复至84.5%。

Conclusion: 形式化表示选择是决定规划可扩展性的关键因素。PDDL2.1的谓词基础表示在LLMs需保持谓词、效果和目标一致性时变得脆弱，而通用约束满足程序(CP-SAT)表现出更好的鲁棒性。状态感知修复策略能有效处理执行时间约束的动态更新。

Abstract: LLMs can plan by either generating action sequences directly as a Planner or translating tasks into domain specific language for an external solver as a Formalizer. While most real-world tasks are asynchronous with non-uniform durations, concurrency, and execution-time constraints, existing benchmarks hardly cover them. We unify these asynchronous planning challenges under a single formulation and introduce the first three benchmarks that address each at scale. We conclude that the choice of formal representation primarily determines whether planning scales: as dependency graphs grow from 5 to 100 actions, Planner collapses from 96% to 5% plan accuracy and PDDL2.1 Formalizer from 13% to 0%, while CP-SAT Formalizer averages 94% and still achieves 83% at 100 actions. Faithfulness diagnostics show that PDDL2.1's predicate-based planning representation becomes brittle compared to general constraint satisfaction programs, when LLMs must keep predicates, effects, and goals consistent. Execution-time updates of planning constraints further degrade performance sharply (Planner 23.9%, PDDL2.1 0.7%, CP-SAT 46.1%), but a state-aware repair strategy that updates only event-induced constraints recovers CP-SAT Formalizer to 84.5%.

</details>


### [128] [A Registry-Bound LLM Pipeline for Evidence-Grounded Trait Extraction across Tropical Plants, Aquatic Species, and Exotic Pets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00994)
*Jeff Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于大语言模型的提取流程，该流程通过版本化的封闭词汇特质注册表、逐行证据引用、置信度标签和多版本保存四个机制，实现了大规模、可审计、基于证据的结构化特质记录提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要从大量出版物中规模化提取结构化特质信息，同时确保提取结果可审计、有证据支持，以解决传统手动提取方法效率低下和可信度验证困难的问题。

Method: 采用包含四个关键机制的LLM提取流水线：1) 版本化的39键封闭词汇特质注册表，约束所有允许值符合类型化模式；2) 逐行原文证据引用，将每个值与源文本链接；3) 逐行置信度标签（高/中，低置信度在持久化前丢弃）；4) 多版本保存机制。

Result: 应用于409,880个可发布的物种，流程执行706,220次运行，持久化5,489,881条特质记录，覆盖409,820个物种(99.985%)，其中81.57%为高置信度。三层验证显示：证据承载行中90.12%的引用为源文本子串；对n=100分层非红区行审核支持率100%；对n=50红区行面效度审核50/50接受。

Conclusion: 本研究的主要贡献是四机制框架，而非声明单条记录的正确性（100%仍需人工审核）。该框架为大规模、证据驱动的结构化信息提取提供了可审计的解决方案。

Abstract: We describe a registry-bound large-language-model extraction pipeline producing evidence-grounded structured trait records at scale, on cultivated tropical plant, aquatic, and pet species. Four mechanisms render LLM-derived rows auditable: a versioned 39-key closed-vocabulary trait registry constraining every admitted value to a typed schema; a per-row verbatim evidence quote tying each value to source text; a per-row confidence label (high or medium; low dropped pre-persist); and multi-version preservation. Applied to 409,880 publishable species from the Tropical Species Encyclopedia, the pipeline executed 706,220 runs and persisted 5,489,881 trait records across 409,820 species (99.985%), 81.57% at high confidence. We report three validation layers in descending evidentiary strength: at full population, 90.12% of 5,427,588 evidence-bearing rows have their quote as a verbatim source substring (93.49% excluding one compliance meta-trait); a quote-supports-value audit on n=100 stratified non-red-zone rows yielded 100/100 (lower bound 96.30%); face-validity on n=50 red-zone rows yielded 50/50 Accept (lower bound 92.86%). Per-record correctness is not claimed; 100% pending human curation. The contribution is the four-mechanism framework.

</details>


### [129] [Decoding in Order-Agnostic Language Models: Chain-Rule Deviation and Uniform Spreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00997)
*Lin Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OALMs（包括dLLMs）在任意揭示顺序下预测掩码标记，但研究发现条件概率并非联合分布的精确分解，揭示顺序变化会导致目标对数似然偏移，置信度方差可作为解码路径的诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究OALMs（如dLLMs）在任意揭示顺序下生成或评分序列的能力，但发现其学习到的条件概率存在不一致性，需要开发诊断工具来评估不同解码路径的质量。

Method: 1. 分析LLaDA-2.1中条件概率的一致性，测量不同揭示顺序对目标对数似然的影响
2. 研究置信度优先（CF）解码的揭示顺序模式
3. 提出基于置信度轨迹形状的诊断方法，利用均匀扩散定理推导目标可恢复性最大化的条件
4. 使用$\mathrm{Var}(\log q_t)$作为比较解码路径的诊断指标
5. 在C4数据集和四个下游基准上验证方法

Result: 1. 条件概率不是连贯联合分布的精确分解：仅改变揭示顺序会使目标对数似然偏移高达0.49 nats/标记
2. 置信度优先解码的揭示顺序在内容标记上接近从左到右
3. 置信度方差能区分结构化路径与随机排序，且与下游正确性一致相关
4. 均匀扩散定理表明，在固定总似然下，当每步置信度均匀分布时目标可恢复性最大化

Conclusion: OALMs的条件概率存在路径依赖的伪影，置信度方差可作为有效的诊断工具来比较解码路径，建议在比较OALM解码路径时同时报告平均置信度和置信度方差。

Abstract: Order-agnostic language models (OALMs), including discrete diffusion language models (dLLMs), are trained to predict masked tokens under arbitrary conditioning sets, allowing sequences to be generated or scored under arbitrary reveal orders at inference time. In LLaDA-2.1, we report three findings. First, the learned conditionals are not exact factorizations of a coherent joint distribution: changing only the reveal order shifts target log-likelihood by up to 0.49 nats/token, so likelihood alone mixes content difficulty with path-dependent artifacts. Second, although confidence-first (CF) decoding is order-agnostic, its reveal orders are close to left-to-right (L2R) on content tokens. Third, we propose a complementary diagnostic based on the shape of the confidence trace. A uniform-spreading theorem shows that, at fixed total likelihood, target recoverability is maximized when per-step confidence is spread uniformly; the resulting deviation motivates $\mathrm{Var}(\log q_t)$ as a diagnostic for comparing decoding paths. Across C4 and four downstream benchmarks, low variance separates structured paths from random ordering, and variance is consistently associated with downstream correctness. These results support reporting mean confidence and confidence variance jointly when comparing OALM decoding paths.

</details>


### [130] [PolySpeech-100: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Speech Understanding Across 100+ Languages and Dialects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01016)
*Sicheng Yang,Shulan Ruan,Shiwei Wu,Yu Liu,Lu Fan,Zhi Li,You He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PolySpeech-100是一个大规模多语言语音理解基准，覆盖110种语言变体，评估端到端语音大语言模型的"母语级"语音理解能力，发现开放源码模型在方言处理上优于级联系统，但在低资源语言上表现较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前端到端语音大语言模型的评估方法仍然局限于简单转录时代，存在三个关键局限：明显偏向高资源语言、关注低层级识别而非语义推理、忽视地区方言。需要建立一个能评估"母语级"语音理解的基准。

Method: 采用创新的混合构建流程，通过黄金标准人类录音和指令驱动合成语音相结合的方式，覆盖19种不同的汉语方言和80多种低资源语言。在PolySpeech-100基准上评估了22个最先进模型（包括Gemini-3、GPT-Audio和Qwen2.5-Omni）。

Result: 1) 开源端到端模型在重方言处理上优于级联系统，证明直接音频处理能保留标准转录中常丢失的关键副语言线索和韵律特征；2) 商业模型保持稳健性，但开源模型在低资源语言上出现灾难性性能下降；3) 在标准零样本设置下，思维链提示通常降低大多数模型的语音理解性能，揭示了当前架构中的模态对齐差距。

Conclusion: PolySpeech-100为下一代包容性、全能型语音大语言模型建立了严格标准，揭示了端到端模型在处理方言方面的优势，同时指出了开源模型在低资源语言上的局限性以及思维链提示的潜在问题。

Abstract: While End-to-End (E2E) Speech-Large Language Models (Speech-LLMs) are rapidly evolving, their evaluation methodologies remain limited to the era of simple transcription. Existing benchmarks suffer from three critical limitations: a pronounced bias towards high-resource languages, a focus on low-level recognition (ASR) rather than semantic reasoning, and a neglect of regional dialects. To bridge this gap, we introduce PolySpeech-100, a massive-scale benchmark designed to assess `native-level' speech comprehension across 110 linguistic variants. We employ a novel hybrid construction pipeline that augments gold-standard human recordings with instruction-driven synthetic speech, allowing us to cover 19 distinct Chinese dialects and over 80 low-resource languages. Extensive evaluation of 22 state-of-the-art models (including Gemini-3, GPT-Audio, and Qwen2.5-Omni) yields pivotal insights. First, we demonstrate that open-source E2E models outperform Cascade (ASR+LLM) systems on heavy dialects, proving that direct audio processing preserves critical paralinguistic cues and prosodic features (e.g., intonation, stress) that are often lost in standard transcription. Second, we reveal a significant performance gap: while commercial models maintain robustness, open-source models suffer catastrophic degradation on low-resource languages. Finally, counter-intuitively, we observe that under standard zero-shot settings, Chain-of-Thought prompting frequently degrades speech understanding performance for most evaluated models, revealing a potential modality alignment gap in current architectures. PolySpeech-100 establishes a rigorous standard for the next generation of inclusive, omni-capable Speech-LLMs. The data, demo, and code are publicly available at https://github.com/YoungSeng/PolySpeech-100.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Position Paper: Post-Solve Robustness in Decision Engines: Feasible Regions and Smoothness Under Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00002)
*Yi-Xiang Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出在优化决策系统中增加一个"求解后鲁棒性层"，用于评估已求解的MILP方案在实际部署中的鲁棒性，包括参数扰动下的可行性和最优性保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MILP决策引擎输出的名义最优解在实际部署中经常因成本、需求或资源可用性的微小扰动而失效，导致可行性问题或解的不连续跳变。当前优化系统缺乏对已求解方案鲁棒性的系统性评估，这是工业应用中的一个关键缺陷。

Method: 提出两个核心概念：1) ε-近似最优可行邻域，量化参数扰动下解保持可行且近似最优的范围；2) 解空间平滑性，评估决策空间中具有小组合编辑的邻近解的竞争力。综合敏感性分析、稳定性分析、鲁棒优化、邻域搜索、对抗测试和学习增强等方法，构建统一的求解后鲁棒性评估框架。

Result: 提出了一个包含四个核心组件的求解后鲁棒性层：1) 围绕当前解的认证内近似；2) 具有校准不确定性的概率鲁棒性估计；3) 对抗鲁棒性边界；4) 与求解器验证对齐的学习型预测和解释。提供了简洁的报告模板和评估协议。

Conclusion: 求解后鲁棒性层是当前优化管道中缺失的关键环节，应将鲁棒性作为决策引擎的一类输出。该框架不取代鲁棒优化或随机规划，而是作为补充层对已求解方案进行审计，为实际部署提供可验证的鲁棒性证据。

Abstract: Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) decision engines routinely output nominally optimal plans for high-stakes industrial systems. Yet deployment rarely matches solve-time assumptions: small perturbations in costs, demands, or resource availability can invalidate feasibility or trigger discontinuous shifts to qualitatively different solutions. We argue that this post-solve robustness gap is a missing layer in today's optimization pipelines and a missing evaluation dimension for learning-enabled decision systems. Rather than replacing robust optimization or stochastic programming, the proposed layer audits a solved incumbent and returns solver-backed evidence about how far that solution can be trusted. We formalize two central objects: (i) an $ε$-near-optimal feasible neighborhood in parameter space, capturing when an incumbent remains feasible and near-optimal under perturbations, and (ii) solution smoothness in decision space, capturing whether nearby alternatives with small combinatorial edits remain competitive. We then synthesize the most relevant partial answers from sensitivity and stability analysis, robust optimization, neighborhood search, adversarial testing, and learning-based enhancements, and articulate an agenda for a unified post-solve robustness layer. Concretely, we call for certified inner approximations around the incumbent, probabilistic robustness estimation with calibrated uncertainty, adversarial robustness margins, and learning-based prediction and explanation aligned with solver-backed verification. We conclude with a compact reporting template and evaluation protocol that would make robustness a first-class output of decision engines.

</details>


### [132] [Emergent Collaborative Deliberation in Multi-Model AI Systems: A BFT-Derived Protocol for Epistemic Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00005)
*VD Doske*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Consilium协议，一种基于拜占庭容错的多模型AI审议架构，将模型间分歧视为认知信号而非错误，通过角色工程和样本验证框架降低成本并发现RLHF训练产生的认知盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI模型间的分歧通常被视为错误而非信息源，且昂贵的尖端模型成本限制了大规模审议。需要一种能利用模型间差异作为认知信号、降低成本并揭示RLHF对齐训练中系统性认知偏见的框架。

Method: 采用拜占庭容错衍生架构，为语言模型分配工程化认知角色，分离模型身份与推理方式。引入来自量化金融的In-Sample/Out-of-Sample验证框架，区分训练数据共识与经验基础结论。进行1,478次审议会话，涵盖10个领域的32个主题。

Result: 1) 认知角色而非底层模型决定认知行为，廉价模型(0.0002美元/批)与前沿模型(10.69美元)产出相当；2) RLHF训练产生可测量的领域特定认知盲点，争议政策主题比确定科学主题的对抗挑战少12.3%，AI安全主题存在不对称偏见；3) 协议本身无方向性偏见；4) Out-of-sample证据检索验证了239个主张并发现167个认知盲点；运行间可重复性为±2.2%标准差；总成本217美元。

Conclusion: Consilium协议证明了低成本多模型审议的可行性，将模型分歧转化为认知信号，有效揭示RLHF训练的认知盲点。该架构具有实际应用价值，已发布MIT许可协议供独立验证。

Abstract: We present the Consilium Protocol, a Byzantine Fault Tolerance-derived architecture for structured multi-model AI deliberation that treats inter-model disagreement as epistemic signal rather than error. The protocol assigns engineered cognitive personas to language models -- separating what a model is from how it reasons -- and introduces an In-Sample/Out-of-Sample validation framework adapted from quantitative finance to distinguish training-data consensus from empirically grounded conclusions. Across 1,478 deliberation sessions spanning 32 topics in 10 domain categories, we demonstrate that (1) the cognitive persona, not the underlying model, determines epistemic behavior: free edge-inference models costing 0.0002 USD per batch produced comparable analytical output to frontier models costing 10.69 USD; (2) RLHF alignment training creates measurable, domain-specific epistemic blind spots -- contested policy topics exhibit 12.3 percentage points less adversarial challenge than settled science topics, and AI safety topics show asymmetric bias ($Δ$=11.6%) where models challenge claims that AI is dangerous far more vigorously than claims that AI risk is overstated; (3) the protocol exhibits no directional bias of its own (immigration $Δ$=2.3%, renewables $Δ$=1.2%); and (4) out-of-sample evidence retrieval validated 239 claims with 100% evidence retrieval and surfaced 167 blind-spot discoveries invisible to training-data deliberation. Run-to-run reproducibility across randomized model$\times$persona assignments averages $\pm$2.2% standard deviation. Total cost for the complete battery including all overhead: 217 USD. We release the protocol specification under MIT license to enable independent verification.

</details>


### [133] [Deliberative Curation: A Protocol for Multi-Agent Knowledge Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00007)
*Steven Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个用于AI代理协作知识库的审议式治理协议，包含知识生命周期、声誉加权投票和分级制裁三层机制，在对抗性环境下相比多数投票显著提升了系统韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理从独立工具转变为共享知识生态系统中的协作参与者，集体知识治理成为关键挑战。人类平台治理机制无法直接移植：代理无状态性破坏了基于威慑的制裁，模型同质性违反了群体智慧独立性假设，谄媚行为破坏了审议共识。

Method: 提出了三层治理协议：1）将知识工件生命周期形式化为标记转换系统；2）集成Beta Reputation和EigenTrust放大的声誉加权审议投票；3）针对无状态代理的分级制裁，包括区分故障和对抗行为的坏代理处理机制。通过基于代理的模拟进行评估，涉及100个代理的7种行为原型和两种对抗场景。

Result: 协议在良性条件下以适度精度为代价，在对抗性环境下显著提升了韧性：中等对抗下精度为0.826 vs 0.791（多数投票），压力下为0.807 vs 0.740（p<0.001）。协议性能下降速度比多数投票慢约三倍。消融分析显示提交-揭示投票隐藏是最有影响力的组件（精度提升8.2-8.6pp，p<0.001），超过声誉加权和审议的总效果。分级制裁在模拟中未触发，仍需实证验证。

Conclusion: 该审议式治理协议为AI代理协作知识库提供了有效的治理框架，在对抗性环境中表现出显著优越性，特别是在投票隐藏机制方面效果突出，但分级制裁机制需要进一步实证验证。

Abstract: As AI agents transition from isolated tools to collaborative participants in shared knowledge ecosystems, governing collective knowledge curation becomes a critical challenge. Human platform governance mechanisms do not transfer directly: agent statelessness undermines deterrence-based sanctions, model homogeneity violates independence assumptions underlying crowd wisdom, and sycophancy collapses deliberative consensus.
  We propose a deliberative curation protocol combining three governance layers: (1) a knowledge artifact lifecycle formalized as a labeled transition system; (2) reputation-weighted deliberative voting integrating Beta Reputation with EigenTrust amplification; and (3) graduated sanctions adapted for stateless agents, including broken agent handling distinguishing malfunction from adversarial behavior.
  We evaluate the protocol through agent-based simulation with 100 agents across seven behavioral archetypes under two adversity scenarios (30 seeds, paired t-tests). The protocol trades modest precision under benign conditions for substantially better resilience under adversity: 0.826 vs 0.791 for majority vote under moderate adversity (p<0.001), widening to 0.807 vs 0.740 under stress (p<0.001). The protocol degrades roughly three times more slowly than majority vote. Ablation analysis identifies commit-reveal vote concealment as the most impactful single component (8.2-8.6pp precision improvement, p<0.001), outperforming reputation weighting and deliberation combined. Graduated sanctions were not exercised in simulation and remain empirically unvalidated.

</details>


### [134] [Agents on a Tree: Pathwise Coordination for Multi-Objective Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00008)
*Jia Zhang,Tengfei Ma,Tianle Li,Daojian Zeng,Xieping Gao,Xiangxiang Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ATOM提出了一种基于树状结构搜索的多智能体框架，用于多目标分子优化，通过专业智能体协调实现多样权衡方案的探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依赖单一策略或固定标量化，难以表示多样化权衡方案和探索多个有希望的设计轨迹，因为早期设计决策会强烈约束下游结果。

Method: ATOM将分子优化构建为树状搜索结构，每个节点对应原子操作并配备专门化智能体，这些智能体沿不同路径协调而不是强制全局共识，通过全局记忆支持跨目标的平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在涉及活性、合成性和ADMET相关性质的挑战性多目标基准测试中，ATOM在帕累托覆盖率和超体积方面一致优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 结果表明，路径式多智能体协调对分子优化是有效的，树状结构交互能够处理分子设计中固有的长时程依赖关系。

Abstract: Multi-objective molecular optimization requires searching vast chemical spaces under conflicting objectives, where early design decisions strongly constrain downstream outcomes. Existing methods typically rely on a single policy or fixed scalarization, which limits their ability to represent diverse trade-offs and to explore multiple promising design trajectories. We propose ATOM, a multi-agent framework that formulates molecular optimization as a tree-structured search. Each node corresponds to an atomic operation and hosts an agent specialized for a particular objective or decision context. Agents coordinate along different paths of the tree rather than enforcing a global consensus, enabling the method to maintain and compare alternative molecular evolution trajectories. A global memory of past optimization behaviors further supports balanced exploration and exploitation across objectives. This tree-structured interaction enables reasoning over long-horizon dependencies inherent in molecular design. Experiments on challenging multi-objective benchmarks involving activity, synthesizability, and ADMET-related properties show that ATOM consistently achieves improved Pareto coverage and hypervolume over strong baselines. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of pathwise multi-agent coordination for molecular optimization. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ATOM-41CE.

</details>


### [135] [Optimal Transport-based Permutation-Invariant Bayesian Optimization of Offshore Wind Farm Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00009)
*Antonio Candelieri,Laurens Bliek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于最优传输理论的置换不变贝叶斯优化方法(PIBO)，用于解决具有对称性的昂贵黑盒优化问题，特别是海上风电场的布局优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯优化(BO)无法利用目标问题可能存在的对称性。特别是在最优布局问题中，决策变量是连续空间中的有限点集，点的顺序不影响目标函数值(如海上风电场布局优化中，交换任意两个相同风力机的位置不影响年发电量)。

Method: 基于最优传输理论，提出置换不变贝叶斯优化方法(PIBO)，将布局优化问题转化为点云优化问题，并通过对称性约束减少搜索空间维度。

Result: PIBO方法相比传统BO方法能提供更好的风电场布局方案，同时计算时间减少约一半。

Conclusion: PIBO是一种有效的置换不变贝叶斯优化方法，特别适用于具有对称性的昂贵黑盒优化问题，如风电场布局优化等工业应用场景。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) is widely and successfully adopted for solving optimization problems having an expensive-to-evaluate, black-box, and non-convex objective function. However, the vanilla BO algorithm is not able to exploit possible symmetries characterizing the target problem. An intuitive case is given by optimal location problems, whose decision variables refer to a finite set of points within a continuous space, with the order of points not affecting the value of the objective function. We refer to this setting as optimization over layouts to distinguish from optimization over point-clouds where, instead, the order of points counts. As an instance of optimization over layouts we consider a real-life industrial-relevant application, that is the optimization of the layout of an offshore wind farm: given identical wind turbines, switching any pair of them has not any effect on the annual energy production. Based on Optimal Transport theory, we propose a Permutation-Invariant BO approach, namely PIBO, proved to provide better wind farm layouts when compared to the vanilla BO approach while cutting computation time roughly in half.

</details>


### [136] [MindGames Arena Generalization Track: In2AI Solution with Delayed Per-Step Reward Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00017)
*Aliaksei Korshuk,Alexander Buyantuev,Ilya Makarov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多智能体战略交互的语言模型智能体训练方法，通过延迟逐步奖励归因和资格门控技术，解决了多智能体环境中奖励分配的难题，在MindGames Arena基准测试中取得了优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体战略交互中训练语言模型智能体面临核心难题：任何动作的质量都依赖于未来可能不会发生的事件、违反游戏规则的移动或其他玩家的决策。标准强化学习假设可以在每个步骤分配奖励，但在结果在时间和智能体之间纠缠的设置中这一假设失效。

Method: 引入了延迟逐步奖励归因与资格门控技术，包括：在回合结束时计算奖励，根据任务特定语义将奖励传播回原始步骤，从训练中排除缺乏有效依赖信息的步骤。结合异步回放生成（通过vLLM的连续批处理）、基于课程的对手采样和多层分层批量构建。

Result: 在NeurIPS 2025的MindGames Arena基准测试中，使用该方法训练的单个8B参数开源模型在对抗性对局中匹配或超越了包括GPT-5在内的显著更大的专有系统，并在开放（无限制）和高效（<=8B参数）两个赛道上获得第一名。

Conclusion: 提出的延迟逐步奖励归因与资格门控方法结合异步回放生成和课程对手采样，能够实现多智能体环境中稳定、样本高效的强化学习训练，使较小参数模型能够超越大型专有系统。

Abstract: Training language model agents for multi-agent strategic interaction presents a core difficulty: the quality of any action may depend on future events that never materialize, on moves that violate game rules, or on decisions made by other players. Standard reinforcement learning assumes that rewards can be assigned at each step, but this assumption fails in settings where outcomes are entangled across time and agents. We introduce delayed per-step reward attribution with eligibility gating, an episode lifecycle and postprocessing pipeline that computes rewards only at episode end, propagates them back to originating steps according to task-specific semantics, and excludes steps that lack valid dependent information from training. Together with asynchronous rollout generation via vLLM's continuous batching, curriculum-based opponent sampling, and multi-level stratified batch construction, this approach enables stable, sample-efficient RL training in multi-agent environments. We evaluate on the MindGames Arena benchmark at NeurIPS 2025, where a single 8-billion-parameter open-source model trained with our method matched or surpassed substantially larger proprietary systems, including GPT-5, in head-to-head play and took first place in both the Open (unrestricted) and Efficient (<=8B parameters) tracks.

</details>


### [137] [Universal Quantum Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00045)
*Sungyong Chung,Alireza Talebpour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 量子计算架构UQT实现精确数学推理，相比经典神经网络具有确定性和计算优势


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典连续空间神经网络难以精确捕捉数学对称性（如模运算、非交换代数），需要大规模参数缩放且存在随机不稳定性，即使出现延迟泛化现象（grokking）后也是如此

Method: 提出通用量子变换器（UQT）架构，基于多量子比特系统的物理特性作为精确数学和代数推理的通用归纳偏置，使用参数化几何相位嵌入和SU(2)波干涉，而不是翻译经典神经机制

Result: UQT在5量子比特基底上完美学习了两个截然不同的形式类：循环模运算（Z11）和非阿贝尔代数（S4置换群）。与经典注意力网络表现出的随机不稳定性不同，UQT实现了数学精确、确定性的泛化（称为"结晶化"现象）。该框架理论上绕过了经典自注意力的二次瓶颈，对数压缩所需表示维度

Conclusion: UQT在NISQ硬件（IBM量子计算机）上证明了可行性，确立了参数化量子拓扑作为精确人工智能的普遍优越物理基底，超越了经典的grokking现象

Abstract: Classical continuous-space neural networks fundamentally struggle to lock into exact mathematical symmetries, such as modular arithmetic and non-commutative algebra. To approximate these discrete logical rules, they often rely on massive parameter scaling, resulting in stochastic instability even after delayed generalization phenomena known as grokking. Here, we introduce the Universal Quantum Transformer (UQT), a fundamentally novel, quantum-native computing architecture that uses the physical properties of multi-qubit systems as a universal inductive bias for exact mathematical and algebraic reasoning. Rather than translating classical neural mechanisms, our framework relies entirely on parameterized geometric phase embedding and $SU(2)$ wave-interference. We demonstrate that the quantum attention circuit, operating on a highly compact 5-qubit substrate, perfectly learns two highly distinct formal classes: cyclic modular arithmetic ($\mathbb{Z}_{11}$) and non-Abelian algebra (the $S_4$ permutation group). While classical attention-based networks exhibit stochastic instability at convergence, the UQT achieves mathematically exact, deterministic generalization. We refer to this phenomenon as crystallization: a step beyond the well-known phenomenon of grokking. Crucially, this framework yields massive computational and memory advantages by theoretically bypassing the quadratic bottleneck of classical self-attention, and by logarithmically compressing the required representation dimension to eliminate the massive over-parameterization inherent to classical networks. Finally, we deploy this architecture on noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) hardware, proving its viability on current IBM Quantum computers. These results establish parameterized quantum topology as a universally superior physical substrate for exact artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [138] [Grokers: Bottom-Up Inductive Comprehension and Write-Time Intelligence over Typed Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00050)
*Gregory Magarshak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Grokers是一种基于类型知识图谱的持久化、结构化理解架构，通过自底向上的归纳遍历依赖子图，将智能处理从查询时转移到写入时，实现零额外LM成本的未来查询响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索增强生成（RAG）方法存在每次查询都需要支付完整理解成本的缺陷，这导致系统效率低下且计算资源浪费。为了解决这一问题，需要设计一种能够在写入阶段建立持久理解、查询阶段无需额外语言模型调用的架构。

Method: Grokers采用自底向上的归纳遍历方法：自治代理分析类型流图中的节点，通过治理的语言模型调用提取结构化属性，并通过依赖关系自下而上组合理解，写入富化后的类型属性。系统提出了三个形式化定理：字节同一性定理确保上下文块在语义变化间的字节一致性；积累单调性定理保证非LM调用交互比例在增长协议下非递减；双重遍历排序定理证明了依赖DAG中自顶向下生成和自底向上理解是各自任务的唯一正确遍历顺序。

Result: Grokers架构实现了近乎100%的KV缓存命中率，建立了确定性的语义搜索替代方案（带有收敛至零LM后备率的同义词缓存协议），并在Qbix/Safebox/Safebots堆栈中提供了开源参考实现。

Conclusion: Grokers通过将智能处理前置到写入时，建立了对类型知识图谱的持久结构化理解，从根本上改变了检索增强系统的计算范式，实现了查询时的零LM成本响应，并通过形式化定理确保了系统的理论基础和性能保证。

Abstract: We present Grokers, an architecture for building persistent, structured comprehension of typed knowledge graphs through bottom-up inductive traversal of dependency subgraphs. Unlike retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), which pays full comprehension cost at every query, Grokers pushes intelligence to write time: autonomous Groker agents analyze nodes in a typed stream graph, extract structured attributes via governed language model (LM) calls, and inductively compose that understanding upward through dependency relations, writing enriched typed attributes that serve all future queries at zero additional LM cost. We prove three formal properties: (1) the Byte-Identity Theorem, establishing that context blocks assembled from a transactionally-maintained denormalization index are byte-identical across LM turns between semantic changes, enabling KV-cache hit rates approaching 100%; (2) the Accumulation Monotonicity Theorem, establishing that the fraction of interactions resolved without LM calls is non-decreasing in the number of completed interactions under a governed wisdom library growth protocol; and (3) the Dual-Traversal Ordering Theorem, establishing that top-down generation and bottom-up comprehension are the unique correct traversal orderings for their respective tasks over a dependency DAG, and that their composition closes into a complete generation-comprehension cycle. We further present a deterministic alternative to embedding-based semantic search, with a synonym caching protocol whose LM fallback rate converges to zero for finite-vocabulary domains. A reference implementation is provided in the open-source Qbix / Safebox / Safebots stack.

</details>


### [139] [Product-Aware Deep Autoencoders for Robust Process Monitoring in Multi-Product Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00052)
*MD Shafikul Islam,Jordan Carden*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 产品感知自编码器通过将学习域限制在特定产品等级分布，相比全局模型能更有效地检测多产品等级制造环境中的异常，特别是在针对性的网络物理攻击场景中显著提升检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业4.0时代，网络物理系统在制造业的集成加速，需要强大的异常检测来确保过程安全。当前数据驱动方法通常使用在全部正常运行数据上训练的"产品无关"全局模型，但现代工业设施经常在不同产品等级下运行。这些全局模型为适应多模态的方差而扩大决策边界，产生了"盲点"，导致细微异常或针对性攻击可能被模型的宽接受区域掩盖。

Method: 提出产品感知自编码器作为原则性缓解方案，将学习域限制在特定产品等级的分布上。该方法与全局无关基线（Global Agnostic baseline）在扩展的田纳西伊士曼过程基准上进行严格验证，通过模拟假设的攻击场景进行压力测试。

Result: 产品感知框架在标准检测指标上与全局基线表现相当，同时提供对特定产品等级操作模式的改进鲁棒性。最关键的是，在模拟假设攻击场景的压力测试中，全局模型在77.8%的场景中未能检测到操作偏差，而产品感知系统实现了100%的检测准确率。

Conclusion: 在灵活制造环境中，通用化异常检测器可能带来非平凡的安全风险，这促使需要向模式感知的诊断架构转变。产品感知方法虽然减少了已识别的盲点风险，但并非所有可能替代方案中的最优缓解措施。

Abstract: As Industry 4.0 accelerates the integration of Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) in manufacturing, robust anomaly detection has become critical for ensuring process safety and security. Current data-driven approaches typically employ "product-agnostic" or global models trained on the aggregate of all normal operating data. However, modern industrial facilities frequently operate under diverse product grades. While computationally simple, these global models inherently expand their decision boundaries to accommodate the variance of multiple modes, creating a "blind spot" where subtle anomalies or targeted cyber-physical attacks may be masked by the wide acceptance region of the model. In this work, we first demonstrate that the vulnerability described above is present in global-agnostic models operating across multiple product grades. We then present a Product-Aware Autoencoder as a principled mitigation that restricts the learning domain to grade-specific distributions. While this approach reduces the identified blind-spot risk, we do not claim it as the optimal mitigation among all possible alternatives. We rigorously validate this approach against a Global Agnostic baseline using the Extended Tennessee Eastman Process (TEP) benchmark. Our empirical results indicate that the Product-Aware framework performs comparably to the global baseline on standard detection metrics, while offering improved robustness to product-grade-specific operating modes. Most critically, stress tests simulating our hypothetical attack scenarios reveal that while the global model fails to detect operational deviations in 77.8% of the scenarios, the product-aware system achieves 100% detection accuracy. These findings suggest that, in flexible manufacturing environments, generalized anomaly detectors can pose non-trivial security risks, motivating a shift toward mode-aware diagnostic architectures.

</details>


### [140] [On the evolution of the concept of probability as a mirror of the evolution of reason](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00102)
*Jean-Louis Le Mouël,Vincent Courtillot,Dominique Gibert,Vladimir Kossobokov,Jean-Baptiste Boulé,Pierpaolo Zuddas,Fernando Lopes,Païkan Marccagi,Alexis Maineult*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文分析了概率论从博弈计算到不确定推理核心框架的演变，并探讨了理性如何超越概率论，纳入模糊逻辑和深度学习来处理概念模糊性和几何预测模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文的动机是理解概率论作为理性形式的演变历程，以及当代科学推理如何需要超越概率论来正式表达不确定性、模糊性和推断。作者认为概率论虽然量化了不确定命题的不确定性，但无法形式化描述这些命题所用概念的模糊性。

Method: 采用历史和认识论分析的方法：1）追溯概率论从帕斯卡、费马到现代贝叶斯推断的发展轨迹；2）将概率论作为信息逻辑的Tarantola观点；3）考察模糊逻辑作为分级意义和定性判断的严格语言；4）分析深度学习作为基于几何插值和优化的独特预测模式。

Result: 分析表明：1）概率论已从机会博弈演变为不确定推理的核心框架，并在现代贝叶斯推断中达到成熟认识论形式；2）概率论存在局限——能量化明确定义命题的不确定性，但无法形式化所用概念的模糊性；3）模糊逻辑能处理概念模糊性和定性判断；4）深度学习提供了基于几何插值和优化的强大预测模式；5）当代科学理性需要明确表达不确定性、模糊性和推断，不能简化为数据驱动性能。

Conclusion: 该文得出结论：当代科学理性是多元框架，概率论、模糊逻辑和深度学习各有其角色和限制。完整的科学推理需要整合概率论（处理不确定性）、模糊逻辑（处理概念模糊性）和深度学习（处理几何模式预测），而不仅仅依赖于数据驱动的性能。真正的科学判断需要明确表达不确定性、模糊性和推断的机制。

Abstract: Over the centuries, probability theory has grown from the calculus of games of chance into a central framework for reasoning under uncertainty. This article interprets that evolution not merely as a mathematical history, but as a transformation of rationality itself. From Pascal and Fermat's combinatorial symmetry to the inductive logic of Bayes and Laplace, from Poisson's statistics of events to Kolmogorov's axiomatic formalization, probability progressively incorporated uncertainty, time, and coherence into scientific judgment. This trajectory reaches a mature epistemological form in modern Bayesian inference, especially in Tarantola's view of probability as a logic of information, where prior knowledge and data are combined coherently. Yet this framework also exposes a limit: probability quantifies uncertainty about well-defined propositions, but does not by itself formalize the vagueness of the concepts used to describe them. The article therefore examines how rationality extends beyond probability. Fuzzy logic is presented as a rigorous language for graded meaning and qualitative judgment, while deep learning is analyzed as a distinct, powerful mode of prediction based on geometric interpolation and optimization rather than explicit inference. By situating probability, fuzzy logic, and deep learning in a common historical and epistemological perspective, the article clarifies their roles and limits. It argues that contemporary scientific rationality cannot be reduced to data-driven performance alone, but requires the explicit articulation of uncertainty, vagueness, and inference.

</details>


### [141] [Evaluating Interactive Reasoning in Large Language Models: A Hierarchical Benchmark with Executable Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00103)
*Mingyuan Fan,Weiguang Han,Daixin Wang,Cen Chen,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个多轮交互推理评估框架，将推理视为主动证据获取和信念更新的过程，并基于该框架构建了包含474个可执行游戏的基准测试，评估前沿LLM在多个维度的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推理评估通常基于静态、一次性输入，无法真实评估LLM在复杂环境中进行主动信息收集、部分观察整合和动态决策的能力。需要一种更符合真实世界推理过程的评估方法。

Method: 设计了多轮交互推理评估框架：LLM只接收任务规则，必须向隐藏环境发出有针对性的查询，随时间整合部分观察结果，并决定何时提交最终答案。评估维度包括标准成功率、交互效率、上下文鲁棒性（通过受控上下文扰动）和元认知适应能力（通过反事实修正和必要性判断）。构建了包含474个可执行游戏的基准，每个游戏在五个固定配置搜索空间（对应五个难度级别）下评估。

Result: 基准测试具有高度区分性：不仅暴露了成功率的巨大差异，还揭示了交互效率的显著不同。上下文扰动导致中等但一致的性能下降，而反事实修正和必要性判断导致更大的性能下降。不同LLM在这些评估维度上表现出明显差异。

Conclusion: 该多轮交互推理评估框架提供了一种更全面、更真实的LLM推理能力评估方法，能够揭示传统评估方法无法检测的能力差异，特别是涉及主动信息收集、动态信念更新和元认知适应的能力。框架的基准测试为LLM推理能力的发展提供了有价值的评估工具。

Abstract: We introduce a multi-turn interactive framework for reasoning evaluation that treats reasoning as active evidence acquisition and belief updating. Wherein, LLMs receive only the task rules, must issue targeted queries to a hidden environment, integrate partial observations over time, and decide when to submit a final answer. Beyond standard success rate and interaction efficiency, we evaluate contextual robustness under controlled contextual perturbations, and metacognitive adaptation through counterfactual revision and necessity judgment. We instantiate the framework as a benchmark of 474 executable games, each evaluated under five fixed configuration search spaces corresponding to five difficulty levels, and evaluate a broad set of frontier LLMs. Results show that the benchmark is highly discriminative, exposing large differences not only in success rate but also in interaction efficiency. Moreover, we empirically show that contextual perturbations cause moderate but consistent declines, whereas counterfactual revision and necessity judgment lead to much larger drops.

</details>


### [142] [A Multi-AI-agent Framework Enabling End-to-end Finite Element Analysis for Solid Mechanics Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00138)
*Titu Ranjan Sarker,Muhammed Jawaad Zulqernine,Ling Yue,Shaowu Pan,Chenxi Wang,Shiyao Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AbaqusAgent是一个基于大语言模型的多智能体框架，用于固体力学分析，能够将用户的自然语言指令转化为有限元分析执行和结果可视化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限元分析存在学习曲线陡峭、初学者易出错的问题，需要多年工程经验才能正确使用。为了解决FEA中边界条件、载荷工况等关键组件定义错误导致的模拟误差，以及降低计算力学教育的门槛

Method: 开发了AbaqusAgent多智能体框架，包含六个智能体：解释器、架构师、输入文件编写器、运行器、审核器和可视化器，涵盖FEA分析的所有关键预处理和后处理步骤

Result: 在50个固体力学问题上成功验证，总体成功率86%。该系统能够将自然语言指令转化为完整的FEA分析和可视化结果

Conclusion: AbaqusAgent不仅提高了固体力学FEA的效率，降低了计算力学教育门槛，还推进了人机交互范式，能够与AI驱动的优化和材料表征工作流程集成

Abstract: Finite element analysis (FEA) is the most important numerical approach for solid mechanics. Challenges of FEA include a steep learning curve for entry-level users and potential false simulations due to incorrect definitions of key simulation components, such as boundary conditions, load cases, and solution variables. Years of engineering experience are usually necessary for real-world problem-solving. To address these issues, we present AbaqusAgent, a multi-agent framework grounded in large language models (LLMs) for solid mechanics analyses. AbaqusAgent is developed to facilitate analysis case generation and execution using Abaqus, one of the most widely used FEA packages, by turning users' natural-language instructions into executed FEA analyses and result visualization. AbaqusAgent is composed of six agents, including interpreter, architect, input writer, runner, reviewer, and visualizer agents, encompassing all the essential pre-processing and post-processing steps of standard FEA analyses. A wide variety of 50 solid mechanics problems have been successfully validated, achieving an overall success rate of 86%. Beyond improving the efficiency of FEA for solid mechanics problems and lowering the barrier to computational mechanics education, AbaqusAgent advances the human-simulation interaction paradigm and enables integration with AI-empowered optimization and material characterization workflows. The code is available at https://github.com/LIRAM-LIN/AbaqusAgent

</details>


### [143] [CAST: Non-Privileged Clipped Asymmetric Self-Teaching with Advantage Flipping for GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00172)
*Yang Li,Gongle Xue,Yijia Guo,Yuheng Yuan,Liwen Hu,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAST是一种针对GRPO式强化学习与可验证奖励的无答案自蒸馏方法，通过停梯度的自教师机制在令牌级别塑造优势函数，解决了传统方法中稀疏奖励和全正确/全错误组梯度消失的问题，提升了数学推理任务的训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两个主要问题：1) GRPO中的结果级奖励只提供稀疏监督，当所有采样轨迹都是正确或错误时，组相对优势会消失；2) OPSD提供密集的令牌级指导，但其令牌偏好与轨迹正确性不一定对齐，且在正确和不正确轨迹上的信号表现不同。需要一种既保持可验证奖励的GRPO目标，又能提供有效令牌级指导的方法。

Method: CAST提出一个无答案的自蒸馏方法：1) 保留GRPO的可验证奖励目标；2) 使用停梯度的自教师机制根据轨迹正确性塑造令牌级优势；3) 与先前方法不同，CAST不需要参考解决方案条件化的教师评分；4) 在整个训练过程中保持自教师对数概率差距激活；5) 应用双向局部优势符号反转：正确轨迹中的教师负令牌可获得负令牌级优势，错误轨迹中的教师正令牌可获得有界正局部优势；6) 对于零方差的完全正确和完全错误组，分配有界符号约束的基础优势。

Result: 在数学推理任务上的实验表明，CAST能够改进RLVR训练，同时保持轻量级的、基于可验证器的轨迹级目标。该方法解决了传统GRPO中的稀疏监督问题和全正确/全错误组的梯度消失问题。

Conclusion: CAST作为一种新颖的无答案自蒸馏方法，成功地将令牌级指导与可验证奖励的GRPO框架相结合，通过创新的优势函数设计解决了现有方法的关键局限性，为基于强化学习的语言模型推理训练提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), especially Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has been widely used to improve reasoning in large language models. However, outcome-level rewards provide only sparse supervision, and group-relative advantages vanish when all sampled trajectories for a prompt are either correct or incorrect. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) offers dense token-level guidance, but its token preferences are not necessarily aligned with trajectory correctness; empirical diagnostics show that OPSD signals behave differently on correct and incorrect rollouts, with teacher-positive and teacher-negative gap signals exhibiting different noise profiles. These diagnostics are conducted under an OPSD-style privileged teacher context for analysis only, whereas CAST training uses answer-free self-teacher scoring.Motivated by these observations, this work proposes CAST, an answer-free self-distillation method for GRPO-style RLVR. CAST keeps the verifier-grounded GRPO objective, but uses a stop-gradient self-teacher to shape token-level advantages according to trajectory correctness. Unlike prior self-distilled RLVR methods, CAST does not require reference-solution-conditioned teacher scoring, keeps the self-teacher log-probability gap active throughout training, and applies bidirectional local advantage sign reversal: teacher-negative tokens in correct trajectories can receive negative token-level advantages, while teacher-positive tokens in incorrect trajectories can receive bounded positive local advantages. For zero-variance all-correct and all-wrong groups, CAST assigns bounded sign-constrained base advantages, so these otherwise zero-gradient groups can contribute verifier-signed token feedback. Experiments on mathematical reasoning show that CAST improves RLVR training while retaining a lightweight, verifier-grounded trajectory-level objective.

</details>


### [144] [TIGER: Traceable Inference with Graph-Based Evidence Routing for Mitigating Hallucinations in Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00232)
*Kaixiang Zhao,Tianrun Yu,Shawn Huang,Porter Jenkins,Yushun Dong,Amanda Hughes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TIGER是一个用于多模态生成的推理时修复框架，它通过独立提取观察图和声明图，并基于图条件风险分数选择高风险声明进行修复，从而减少幻觉内容并保持任务质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时修复方法存在两个主要局限：输出中的幻觉声明会影响模型对输入的解释；自由形式的反馈无法在事实层面进行排序或调度。需要一种能够实现局部化修复的方法。

Method: TIGER框架独立从输入中提取观察图，从当前输出中提取声明图，然后基于支持和冲突为每个声明分配图条件风险分数。模型仅修复选定的高风险声明，同时保持骨干网络冻结。

Result: 在四个跨模态路径（图像到文本、图像+文本到文本、音频到文本、视频到文本）的实验显示，TIGER能够减少不支持的内容，同时保持任务质量。改进效果在多个骨干网络上保持一致，并在多源设置的CrisisFACTS案例研究中显示能提高基础性。

Conclusion: TIGER通过重新设计反馈机制实现局部化修复，提供了收敛分析证明总风险在温和假设下几何递减到明确渐近界，为多模态生成中的事实级修复提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We study fact-level repair for multimodal generation, where a fluent output may contain specific facts that are not supported by the input. Existing inference-time repair methods often generate feedback by jointly conditioning on the input and the current output. This design has two limitations: hallucinated claims in the output can bias the model's interpretation of the input, and free-form feedback cannot be ranked or scheduled at the fact level. We present TIGER, an inference-time framework that redesigns feedback for localized repair. TIGER independently extracts an observation graph from the input and a claim graph from the current output, then assigns each claim a graph-conditioned risk score based on support and conflict. The model repairs selected high-risk claims while keeping the backbone frozen. We provide a convergence analysis showing that the expected total risk decreases geometrically to an explicit asymptotic bound under mild assumptions. Experiments across four cross-modal paths, including image-to-text, image+text-to-text, audio-to-text, and video-to-text, show that TIGER reduces unsupported content while preserving task quality.
